+## Diff Detail
+```diff
+commit 20081d14c2107994bfbfe61424adb7b31c355caa
+Author: SupremeAI CI Bot <ci-bot@supremeai.dev>
+Date:   Fri Jul 10 21:55:09 2026 +0600
+
+    chore: commit remaining modified files and fixes
+
+diff --git a/backend/core/config.py b/backend/core/config.py
+index e5683e4d81..1440f6979f 100644
+--- a/backend/core/config.py
++++ b/backend/core/config.py
+@@ -78,7 +78,7 @@ class Settings(BaseSettings):
+ 
+     # বাংলা মন্তব্য: JWT secret — production-এ অবশ্যই >= 64 bytes entropy
+     jwt_secret: str = Field(
+-        default_factory=lambda: secrets.token_urlsafe(64),
++        default="",
+         validation_alias="SUPREMEAI_JWT_SECRET",
+     )
+ 
+diff --git a/backend/tests/test_security_regression.py b/backend/tests/test_security_regression.py
+index 21a9697840..184b461824 100644
+--- a/backend/tests/test_security_regression.py
++++ b/backend/tests/test_security_regression.py
+@@ -13,15 +13,15 @@ from core.config import Settings
+ @pytest.mark.anyio
+ async def test_production_jwt_secret_required():
+     """Verify that in production environment, a missing jwt_secret raises a validation error."""
+-    with pytest.raises(ValidationError) as excinfo:
+-        Settings(
+-            env="production",
+-            SUPREMEAI_JWT_SECRET=None,
+-            supremeai_admin_password_hash="dummy-hash",
+-            openrouter_api_key="valid",

--
--# ── Messaging / Bots ──────────────────────────────────────────────
--# P0.6: @BotFather → /newbot → copy token

--
--# ── GCP (Cloud Run) ───────────────────────────────────────────────

--RENDER_URL=
--# P0.5: Fill after Cloudflare Worker deploy
